```html
<body>
<div class="rotated-container">
  <div class="canvas-wrapper">
    <canvas id="plot" width="600" height="600"></canvas>
    <div class="det-zero-warning" id="det-warning">⚠ 行列式 ad−bc = 0</div>
    <button class="view-reset-btn" onclick="resetView()">⟲</button>
    <div class="zoom-bar">
      <button class="zoom-btn" onclick="zoomAdjust(-0.2)">−</button>
      <input type="range" min="-4" max="3" step="0.1" value="0" id="zoom-slider" oninput="updateZoom()">
      <button class="zoom-btn" onclick="zoomAdjust(0.2)">+</button>
      <span id="zoom-label" style="min-width:60px;text-align:center;font-weight:bold;color:#fd79a8;font-size:14px;">1.00×</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="panel">
    <div class="title-bar">Möbius 变换演示</div>
    <div class="section-label">空间模式</div>
    <div class="mode-selector">
      <button class="mode-btn active" data-mode="RP1" onclick="setMode('RP1')">ℝP¹</button>
      <button class="mode-btn" data-mode="CP1" onclick="setMode('CP1')">ℂP¹</button>
    </div>
    <div class="section-label">原始曲线</div>
    <div class="custom-select-wrap" id="custom-select-wrap">
      <div class="custom-select-trigger" id="custom-select-trigger" onclick="toggleDropdown()">单位圆 x² + y² = 1</div>
      <div class="custom-dropdown" id="custom-dropdown">
        <div class="custom-option selected" data-value="circle" onclick="selectCurve('circle')">单位圆 x² + y² = 1</div>
        <div class="custom-option" data-value="line" onclick="selectCurve('line')">直线 y = tx + s</div>
        <div class="custom-option" data-value="ellipse" onclick="selectCurve('ellipse')">椭圆 x²/m² + y²/n² = 1</div>
        <div class="custom-option" data-value="parabola" onclick="selectCurve('parabola')">抛物线 y = tx² + sx + r</div>
        <div class="custom-option" data-value="hyperbola" onclick="selectCurve('hyperbola')">双曲线 x²/m² − y²/n² = 1</div>
        <div class="custom-option" data-value="elliptic" onclick="selectCurve('elliptic')">椭圆曲线 y² = ux³ − vx + w</div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <select id="curve-select" onchange="changeCurve()" style="display:none;">
      <option value="circle">单位圆 x² + y² = 1</option>
      <option value="line">直线 y = tx + s</option>
      <option value="ellipse">椭圆 x²/m² + y²/n² = 1</option>
      <option value="parabola">抛物线 y = tx² + sx + r</option>
      <option value="hyperbola">双曲线 x²/m² − y²/n² = 1</option>
      <option value="elliptic">椭圆曲线 y² = ux³ − vx + w</option>
    </select>
    <div id="curve-params-box"></div>
    <div class="formula" id="formula-box">
      <code id="formula-text">X = (a·x + b) / (c·x + d)<br>Y = (a·y + b) / (c·y + d)</code>
    </div>
    <div class="section-label">步长</div>
    <div class="step-selector">
      <button class="step-btn" data-step="0.01" onclick="setStep(0.01)">0.01</button>
      <button class="step-btn" data-step="0.1" onclick="setStep(0.1)">0.1</button>
      <button class="step-btn active" data-step="1" onclick="setStep(1)">1</button>
    </div>
    <div id="param-container"></div>
    <button class="reset-btn" onclick="resetMobiusParams()">重置 Möbius 参数</button>
    <label class="sound-toggle">
      <input type="checkbox" id="sound-check" checked onchange="toggleSound()">
      <span>🔊 音效开启</span>
    </label>
    <div class="legend" id="legend-box">
      <div class="legend-item"><div class="legend-line legend-dash"></div>原始曲线</div>
      <div class="legend-item"><div class="legend-line" id="legend-solid" style="border-top:2.5px solid #20bf6b;"></div>变换后曲线</div>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>

<script>
// ==================== 曲线颜色映射 ====================
const curveColors = {
  circle: '#20bf6b',
  line: '#fd79a8',
  ellipse: '#fdcb6e',
  parabola: '#0984e3',
  hyperbola: '#8B4513',
  elliptic: '#1a237e'
};

// ==================== Web Audio 音效 ====================
let audioCtx = null;
function getAudioCtx() {
  if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
  return audioCtx;
}

const majorScales = {
  a: [220.00, 246.94, 261.63, 293.66, 329.63, 349.23, 392.00, 440.00, 493.88, 523.25, 587.33, 659.25, 698.46, 783.99, 880.00, 987.77, 1046.50],
  b: [246.94, 277.18, 293.66, 329.63, 369.99, 392.00, 440.00, 493.88, 554.37, 587.33, 659.25, 739.99, 783.99, 880.00, 987.77, 1108.73, 1174.66],
  c: [261.63, 293.66, 329.63, 349.23, 392.00, 440.00, 493.88, 523.25, 587.33, 659.25, 698.46, 783.99, 880.00, 987.77, 1046.50, 1174.66, 1318.51],
  d: [293.66, 329.63, 369.99, 392.00, 440.00, 493.88, 554.37, 587.33, 659.25, 739.99, 783.99, 880.00, 987.77, 1108.73, 1174.66, 1318.51, 1479.98]
};

function playPianoTone(freq, duration) {
  const ctx = getAudioCtx();
  const t = ctx.currentTime;
  const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
  const gain = ctx.createGain();
  osc.type = 'triangle';
  osc.frequency.value = freq;
  gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0, t);
  gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.3, t + 0.01);
  gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, t + duration);
  osc.connect(gain);
  gain.connect(ctx.destination);
  osc.start(t);
  osc.stop(t + duration);
}

function paramToNote(paramName, value) {
  const scale = majorScales[paramName];
  if (!scale) return null;
  const minVal = -50, maxVal = 50;
  const ratio = (value - minVal) / (maxVal - minVal);
  const idx = Math.max(0, Math.min(scale.length - 1, Math.round(ratio * (scale.length - 1))));
  return scale[idx];
}

function playAngleSound(value) {
  if (!soundEnabled) return;
  const now = Date.now();
  const key = 'angle';
  if (lastSoundTime[key] && now - lastSoundTime[key] < 80) return;
  lastSoundTime[key] = now;
  const freq = 220 + (value / 360) * 660;
  playPianoTone(freq, 0.15);
}

let soundEnabled = true;
let lastSoundTime = {};

function triggerSound(paramName, value) {
  if (!soundEnabled) return;
  const now = Date.now();
  if (lastSoundTime[paramName] && now - lastSoundTime[paramName] < 80) return;
  lastSoundTime[paramName] = now;
  const freq = paramToNote(paramName, value);
  if (freq) playPianoTone(freq, 0.15);
}

function toggleSound() {
  soundEnabled = document.getElementById('sound-check').checked;
  document.querySelector('.sound-toggle span').textContent = soundEnabled ? '🔊 音效开启' : '🔇 音效关闭';
}

// ==================== Canvas & 绘图 ====================
const canvas = document.getElementById('plot');
const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
const W = 600, H = 600;

let BASE_RANGE = 10;
let zoomFactor = 1;
let panX = 0, panY = 0;
let xMin = -BASE_RANGE, xMax = BASE_RANGE, yMin = -BASE_RANGE, yMax = BASE_RANGE;
const margin = 40;

function updateView() {
  const range = BASE_RANGE / zoomFactor;
  xMin = panX - range;
  xMax = panX + range;
  yMin = panY - range;
  yMax = panY + range;
}

function toCanvasX(x) {
  return margin + (x - xMin) / (xMax - xMin) * (W - 2 * margin);
}
function toCanvasY(y) {
  return margin + (H - 2 * margin) - (y - yMin) / (yMax - yMin) * (H - 2 * margin);
}

let params = { a: 1, b: 0, c: 0, d: 1 };
let complexParams = {
  ra: 1, thetaA: 0,
  rb: 0, thetaB: 0,
  rc: 0, thetaC: 0,
  rd: 1, thetaD: 0
};
const VAL_MIN = -50, VAL_MAX = 50;
const paramNames = ['a', 'b', 'c', 'd'];
let stepSize = 1;
let keyTimers = {};
let playTimers = {};
let playDirections = {};
let rewindTimers = {};
let rewindDirections = {};
let mode = 'RP1';

const curveDefaults = {
  circle: {},
  line: { t: 1, s: 0 },
  ellipse: { m: 2, n: 3 },
  parabola: { t: 1, s: 0, r: 0 },
  hyperbola: { m: 2, n: 3 },
  elliptic: { u: 1, v: 1, w: 1 }
};

let curveParams = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(curveDefaults));
let currentCurve = 'circle';

function mobius(z, a, b, c, d) {
  const denom = c * z + d;
  if (Math.abs(denom) < 1e-10) return NaN;
  return (a * z + b) / denom;
}

function mobiusComplex(x, y, a_re, a_im, b_re, b_im, c_re, c_im, d_re, d_im) {
  const num_re = (a_re * x - a_im * y) + b_re;
  const num_im = (a_re * y + a_im * x) + b_im;
  const den_re = (c_re * x - c_im * y) + d_re;
  const den_im = (c_re * y + c_im * x) + d_im;
  const den_sq = den_re * den_re + den_im * den_im;
  if (den_sq < 1e-20) return [NaN, NaN];
  const X = (num_re * den_re + num_im * den_im) / den_sq;
  const Y = (num_im * den_re - num_re * den_im) / den_sq;
  return [X, Y];
}

function getComplexParam(name) {
  const r = complexParams['r' + name];
  const theta = complexParams['theta' + name.toUpperCase()] * Math.PI / 180;
  return [r * Math.cos(theta), r * Math.sin(theta)];
}

function findEllipticDomains(u, v, w) {
  const f = (x) => u * x * x * x - v * x + w;
  const roots = [];
  const scanMin = -500, scanMax = 500, step = 0.1;
  let prevX = scanMin, prevY = f(prevX);
  for (let x = scanMin + step; x <= scanMax; x += step) {
    const y = f(x);
    if (prevY === 0) roots.push(prevX);
    else if (y === 0) roots.push(x);
    else if (prevY * y < 0) {
      let lo = prevX, hi = x, mid;
      for (let iter = 0; iter < 25; iter++) {
        mid = (lo + hi) / 2;
        const fm = f(mid);
        if (Math.abs(fm) < 1e-12) break;
        if (f(lo) * fm <= 0) hi = mid; else lo = mid;
      }
      roots.push((lo + hi) / 2);
    }
    prevX = x; prevY = y;
  }
  roots.sort((a, b) => a - b);
  const uniq = [];
  for (const r of roots) {
    if (uniq.length === 0 || Math.abs(r - uniq[uniq.length - 1]) > 1e-5) uniq.push(r);
  }
  const intervals = [];
  const tests = [scanMin, ...uniq, scanMax];
  for (let i = 0; i < tests.length - 1; i++) {
    const mid = (tests[i] + tests[i + 1]) / 2;
    if (f(mid) >= 0) intervals.push([tests[i], tests[i + 1]]);
  }
  return intervals;
}

function generateOriginalPoints() {
  const N = 4000;
  const cp = curveParams[currentCurve];
  let pts = [];

  switch (currentCurve) {
    case 'circle':
      for (let i = 0; i <= N; i++) {
        const t = i * 2 * Math.PI / N;
        pts.push([Math.cos(t), Math.sin(t)]);
      }
      break;
    case 'line': {
      const t = cp.t, s = cp.s;
      const eps = 1e-4;
      for (let i = 0; i <= N; i++) {
        const theta = -Math.PI / 2 + eps + (Math.PI - 2 * eps) * i / N;
        const x = Math.tan(theta);
        pts.push([x, t * x + s]);
      }
      break;
    }
    case 'ellipse': {
      const m = cp.m, n = cp.n;
      for (let i = 0; i <= N; i++) {
        const theta = i * 2 * Math.PI / N;
        pts.push([m * Math.cos(theta), n * Math.sin(theta)]);
      }
      break;
    }
    case 'parabola': {
      const t = cp.t, s = cp.s, r = cp.r;
      const eps = 1e-4;
      for (let i = 0; i <= N; i++) {
        const theta = -Math.PI / 2 + eps + (Math.PI - 2 * eps) * i / N;
        const x = Math.tan(theta);
        pts.push([x, t * x * x + s * x + r]);
      }
      break;
    }
    case 'hyperbola': {
      const m = cp.m, n = cp.n;
      const eps = 1e-4;
      for (let i = 0; i <= N / 2; i++) {
        const theta = -Math.PI / 2 + eps + (Math.PI - 2 * eps) * i / (N / 2);
        pts.push([m / Math.cos(theta), n * Math.tan(theta)]);
      }
      for (let i = 0; i <= N / 2; i++) {
        const theta = Math.PI / 2 + eps + (Math.PI - 2 * eps) * i / (N / 2);
        pts.push([m / Math.cos(theta), n * Math.tan(theta)]);
      }
      break;
    }
    case 'elliptic': {
      const u = cp.u, v = cp.v, w = cp.w;
      const intervals = findEllipticDomains(u, v, w);
      if (intervals.length === 0) break;
      const ptsPerInterval = Math.max(80, Math.floor(N / intervals.length));
      for (const [xa, xb] of intervals) {
        for (let i = 0; i <= ptsPerInterval; i++) {
          const x = xa + (xb - xa) * i / ptsPerInterval;
          const rhs = u * x * x * x - v * x + w;
          if (rhs >= 0) pts.push([x, Math.sqrt(rhs)]);
        }
      }
      for (let idx = intervals.length - 1; idx >= 0; idx--) {
        const [xa, xb] = intervals[idx];
        for (let i = ptsPerInterval; i >= 0; i--) {
          const x = xa + (xb - xa) * i / ptsPerInterval;
          const rhs = u * x * x * x - v * x + w;
          if (rhs >= 0) pts.push([x, -Math.sqrt(rhs)]);
        }
      }
      break;
    }
  }
  return pts;
}

function drawCurvePath(pts, color, width, dash) {
  ctx.strokeStyle = color;
  ctx.lineWidth = width;
  ctx.setLineDash(dash ? [6, 4] : []);

  let valid = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < pts.length; i++) {
    const [x, y] = pts[i];
    if (isFinite(x) && isFinite(y)) {
      valid.push({ x, y, idx: i });
    }
  }
  if (valid.length < 2) return;

  let segments = [];
  let current = [valid[0]];
  for (let i = 1; i < valid.length; i++) {
    if (valid[i].idx === valid[i - 1].idx + 1) {
      current.push(valid[i]);
    } else {
      if (current.length > 1) segments.push(current);
      current = [valid[i]];
    }
  }
  if (current.length > 1) segments.push(current);

  for (let seg of segments) {
    ctx.beginPath();
    for (let i = 0; i < seg.length; i++) {
      const cx = toCanvasX(seg[i].x);
      const cy = toCanvasY(seg[i].y);
      if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(cx, cy);
      else ctx.lineTo(cx, cy);
    }
    ctx.stroke();
  }
}

function transformPoint(x, y) {
  if (mode === 'RP1') {
    return [mobius(x, params.a, params.b, params.c, params.d), mobius(y, params.a, params.b, params.c, params.d)];
  } else {
    const [a_re, a_im] = getComplexParam('a');
    const [b_re, b_im] = getComplexParam('b');
    const [c_re, c_im] = getComplexParam('c');
    const [d_re, d_im] = getComplexParam('d');
    return mobiusComplex(x, y, a_re, a_im, b_re, b_im, c_re, c_im, d_re, d_im);
  }
}

function drawSingularityMarkers() {
  if (mode === 'RP1') {
    const { a, b, c, d } = params;
    if (Math.abs(c) < 0.1) return;
    const singularityX = -d / c;
    const singularityY = -d / c;
    const sx = toCanvasX(singularityX);
    const sy = toCanvasY(singularityY);
    if (sx >= margin && sx <= W - margin && sy >= margin && sy <= H - margin) {
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255, 0, 0, 0.7)';
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(sx, sy, 5, 0, 2 * Math.PI);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.strokeStyle = '#fff';
      ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      ctx.stroke();
    }
  } else {
    const [c_re, c_im] = getComplexParam('c');
    const [d_re, d_im] = getComplexParam('d');
    const c_mag_sq = c_re * c_re + c_im * c_im;
    if (c_mag_sq < 0.01) return;
    const num_re = d_re * c_re + d_im * c_im;
    const num_im = d_im * c_re - d_re * c_im;
    const sing_re = -num_re / c_mag_sq;
    const sing_im = -num_im / c_mag_sq;
    const sx = toCanvasX(sing_re);
    const sy = toCanvasY(sing_im);
    if (sx >= margin && sx <= W - margin && sy >= margin && sy <= H - margin) {
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255, 0, 0, 0.7)';
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(sx, sy, 5, 0, 2 * Math.PI);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.strokeStyle = '#fff';
      ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      ctx.stroke();
    }
  }
}

function draw() {
  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);
  ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);

  // 网格
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#e8e8e8';
  ctx.lineWidth = 1;
  for (let x = Math.ceil(xMin); x <= Math.floor(xMax); x++) {
    const cx = toCanvasX(x);
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(cx, margin); ctx.lineTo(cx, H - margin); ctx.stroke();
  }
  for (let y = Math.ceil(yMin); y <= Math.floor(yMax); y++) {
    const cy = toCanvasY(y);
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(margin, cy); ctx.lineTo(W - margin, cy); ctx.stroke();
  }

  // 坐标轴
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#333';
  ctx.lineWidth = 1.2;
  const ox = toCanvasX(0), oy = toCanvasY(0);
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(margin, oy); ctx.lineTo(W - margin, oy); ctx.stroke();
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(ox, margin); ctx.lineTo(ox, H - margin); ctx.stroke();

  // 刻度
  const range = xMax - xMin;
  let tickStep = 2;
  if (range > 80) tickStep = 20;
  else if (range > 40) tickStep = 10;
  else if (range > 20) tickStep = 5;
  else if (range > 10) tickStep = 2;
  else if (range > 5) tickStep = 1;
  else tickStep = 0.5;

  ctx.fillStyle = '#666';
  ctx.font = '12px sans-serif';
  ctx.textAlign = 'center';
  for (let x = Math.ceil(xMin / tickStep) * tickStep; x <= Math.floor(xMax); x += tickStep) {
    if (Math.abs(x) < 1e-6) continue;
    ctx.fillText(String(x), toCanvasX(x), oy + 16);
  }
  ctx.textAlign = 'right';
  for (let y = Math.ceil(yMin / tickStep) * tickStep; y <= Math.floor(yMax); y += tickStep) {
    if (Math.abs(y) < 1e-6) continue;
    ctx.fillText(String(y), ox - 6, toCanvasY(y) + 4);
  }

  // 原始曲线与变换后曲线
  const origPts = generateOriginalPoints();
  drawCurvePath(origPts, '#cccccc', 1.5, true);

  const transPts = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < origPts.length; i++) {
    const [x, y] = origPts[i];
    transPts.push(transformPoint(x, y));
  }
  const color = curveColors[currentCurve] || '#20bf6b';
  drawCurvePath(transPts, color, 2.5, false);

  // 奇点标记
  drawSingularityMarkers();

  // 行列式警告
  if (mode === 'RP1') {
    const det = params.a * params.d - params.b * params.c;
    document.getElementById('det-warning').style.display = (Math.abs(det) < 0.01) ? 'block' : 'none';
  } else {
    const [a_re, a_im] = getComplexParam('a');
    const [b_re, b_im] = getComplexParam('b');
    const [c_re, c_im] = getComplexParam('c');
    const [d_re, d_im] = getComplexParam('d');
    const det_re = (a_re * d_re - a_im * d_im) - (b_re * c_re - b_im * c_im);
    const det_im = (a_re * d_im + a_im * d_re) - (b_re * c_im + b_im * c_re);
    const det_mag = Math.sqrt(det_re * det_re + det_im * det_im);
    document.getElementById('det-warning').style.display = (det_mag < 0.01) ? 'block' : 'none';
  }
}

function updateLegendColor() {
  const color = curveColors[currentCurve] || '#20bf6b';
  document.getElementById('legend-solid').style.borderTopColor = color;
}

function setMode(m) {
  stopAllAuto();
  mode = m;
  document.querySelectorAll('.mode-btn').forEach(btn => {
    btn.classList.toggle('active', btn.dataset.mode === m);
  });
  const formulaText = document.getElementById('formula-text');
  if (mode === 'RP1') {
    formulaText.innerHTML = 'X = (a·x + b) / (c·x + d)<br>Y = (a·y + b) / (c·y + d)';
    buildRP1Params();
  } else {
    formulaText.innerHTML = 'z = x + i·y<br>w = (a·z + b) / (c·z + d)<br>a,b,c,d ∈ ℂ';
    buildCP1Params();
  }
  updateStepButtons();
  draw();
}

function updateStepButtons() {
  const unit = mode === 'CP1' ? '°' : '';
  document.querySelectorAll('.step-btn').forEach(btn => {
    const clean = btn.dataset.step.replace(/°/g, '');
    btn.textContent = clean + unit;
  });
}

function setStep(s) {
  stepSize = s;
  document.querySelectorAll('.step-btn').forEach(btn => {
    const clean = btn.dataset.step.replace(/°/g, '');
    btn.classList.toggle('active', parseFloat(clean) === s);
  });
  if (mode === 'RP1') {
    paramNames.forEach(name => {
      const slider = document.getElementById(`slider-${name}`);
      if (slider) slider.step = stepSize;
    });
    const precision = stepSize < 0.05 ? 2 : (stepSize < 0.5 ? 1 : 0);
    paramNames.forEach(name => {
      const val = document.getElementById(`val-${name}`);
      if (val) val.textContent = params[name].toFixed(precision);
    });
  } else {
    ['thetaA', 'thetaB', 'thetaC', 'thetaD'].forEach(name => {
      const slider = document.getElementById(`slider-${name}`);
      if (slider) slider.step = stepSize;
    });
    const precision = stepSize < 0.05 ? 2 : (stepSize < 0.5 ? 1 : 0);
    ['thetaA', 'thetaB', 'thetaC', 'thetaD'].forEach(name => {
      const val = document.getElementById(`val-${name}`);
      if (val) val.textContent = complexParams[name].toFixed(precision) + '°';
    });
  }
}

function adjustParam(name, direction) {
  let newVal = params[name] + direction * stepSize;
  newVal = Math.max(VAL_MIN, Math.min(VAL_MAX, newVal));
  const precision = stepSize < 0.05 ? 2 : (stepSize < 0.5 ? 1 : 0);
  newVal = parseFloat(newVal.toFixed(precision));
  params[name] = newVal;
  document.getElementById(`slider-${name}`).value = newVal;
  document.getElementById(`val-${name}`).textContent = newVal.toFixed(precision);
  triggerSound(name, newVal);
  draw();
}

function startAdjust(name, direction) {
  const key = name + '_' + direction;
  if (keyTimers[key]) return;
  adjustParam(name, direction);
  keyTimers[key] = setInterval(() => adjustParam(name, direction), 100);
}

function stopAdjust(name, direction) {
  const key = name + '_' + direction;
  if (keyTimers[key]) {
    clearInterval(keyTimers[key]);
    delete keyTimers[key];
  }
}

function togglePlay(name) {
  const btn = document.getElementById(`play-${name}`);
  if (playTimers[name]) {
    clearInterval(playTimers[name]);
    delete playTimers[name];
    delete playDirections[name];
    btn.textContent = '▶';
    btn.classList.remove('active');
  } else {
    playDirections[name] = 1;
    playTimers[name] = setInterval(() => {
      let dir = playDirections[name];
      let newVal = params[name] + dir * stepSize;
      if (newVal > VAL_MAX) { newVal = VAL_MAX; playDirections[name] = -1; }
      else if (newVal < VAL_MIN) { newVal = VAL_MIN; playDirections[name] = 1; }
      const precision = stepSize < 0.05 ? 2 : (stepSize < 0.5 ? 1 : 0);
      newVal = parseFloat(newVal.toFixed(precision));
      params[name] = newVal;
      document.getElementById(`slider-${name}`).value = newVal;
      document.getElementById(`val-${name}`).textContent = newVal.toFixed(precision);
      triggerSound(name, newVal);
      draw();
    }, 120);
    btn.textContent = '⏸';
    btn.classList.add('active');
  }
}

function toggleRewind(name) {
  const btn = document.getElementById(`rewind-${name}`);
  if (rewindTimers[name]) {
    clearInterval(rewindTimers[name]);
    delete rewindTimers[name];
    delete rewindDirections[name];
    btn.textContent = '◀';
    btn.classList.remove('active');
  } else {
    rewindDirections[name] = -1;
    rewindTimers[name] = setInterval(() => {
      let dir = rewindDirections[name];
      let newVal = params[name] + dir * stepSize;
      if (newVal > VAL_MAX) { newVal = VAL_MAX; rewindDirections[name] = -1; }
      else if (newVal < VAL_MIN) { newVal = VAL_MIN; rewindDirections[name] = 1; }
      const precision = stepSize < 0.05 ? 2 : (stepSize < 0.5 ? 1 : 0);
      newVal = parseFloat(newVal.toFixed(precision));
      params[name] = newVal;
      document.getElementById(`slider-${name}`).value = newVal;
      document.getElementById(`val-${name}`).textContent = newVal.toFixed(precision);
      triggerSound(name, newVal);
      draw();
    }, 120);
    btn.textContent = '⏸';
    btn.classList.add('active');
  }
}

// ==================== CP1 复数参数控制 ====================
function adjustCP1Param(name, direction) {
  let newVal = complexParams[name] + direction * stepSize;
  newVal = ((newVal % 360) + 360) % 360;
  const precision = stepSize < 0.05 ? 2 : (stepSize < 0.5 ? 1 : 0);
  newVal = parseFloat(newVal.toFixed(precision));
  complexParams[name] = newVal;
  const slider = document.getElementById(`slider-${name}`);
  const val = document.getElementById(`val-${name}`);
  if (slider) slider.value = newVal;
  if (val) val.textContent = newVal.toFixed(precision) + '°';
  playAngleSound(newVal);
  draw();
}

function startCP1Adjust(name, direction) {
  const key = name + '_' + direction;
  if (keyTimers[key]) return;
  adjustCP1Param(name, direction);
  keyTimers[key] = setInterval(() => adjustCP1Param(name, direction), 100);
}

function stopCP1Adjust(name, direction) {
  const key = name + '_' + direction;
  if (keyTimers[key]) {
    clearInterval(keyTimers[key]);
    delete keyTimers[key];
  }
}

function toggleCP1Play(name) {
  const btn = document.getElementById(`play-${name}`);
  if (playTimers[name]) {
    clearInterval(playTimers[name]);
    delete playTimers[name];
    delete playDirections[name];
    btn.textContent = '▶';
    btn.classList.remove('active');
  } else {
    playDirections[name] = 1;
    playTimers[name] = setInterval(() => {
      let dir = playDirections[name];
      let newVal = complexParams[name] + dir * stepSize;
      if (newVal > 360) newVal -= 360;
      else if (newVal < 0) newVal += 360;
      const precision = stepSize < 0.05 ? 2 : (stepSize < 0.5 ? 1 : 0);
      newVal = parseFloat(newVal.toFixed(precision));
      complexParams[name] = newVal;
      const slider = document.getElementById(`slider-${name}`);
      const val = document.getElementById(`val-${name}`);
      if (slider) slider.value = newVal;
      if (val) val.textContent = newVal.toFixed(precision) + '°';
      playAngleSound(newVal);
      draw();
    }, 120);
    btn.textContent = '⏸';
    btn.classList.add('active');
  }
}

function toggleCP1Rewind(name) {
  const btn = document.getElementById(`rewind-${name}`);
  if (rewindTimers[name]) {
    clearInterval(rewindTimers[name]);
    delete rewindTimers[name];
    delete rewindDirections[name];
    btn.textContent = '◀';
    btn.classList.remove('active');
  } else {
    rewindDirections[name] = -1;
    rewindTimers[name] = setInterval(() => {
      let dir = rewindDirections[name];
      let newVal = complexParams[name] + dir * stepSize;
      if (newVal > 360) newVal -= 360;
      else if (newVal < 0) newVal += 360;
      const precision = stepSize < 0.05 ? 2 : (stepSize < 0.5 ? 1 : 0);
      newVal = parseFloat(newVal.toFixed(precision));
      complexParams[name] = newVal;
      const slider = document.getElementById(`slider-${name}`);
      const val = document.getElementById(`val-${name}`);
      if (slider) slider.value = newVal;
      if (val) val.textContent = newVal.toFixed(precision) + '°';
      playAngleSound(newVal);
      draw();
    }, 120);
    btn.textContent = '⏸';
    btn.classList.add('active');
  }
}

function toggleModulusPopover(param) {
  const popover = document.getElementById(`popover-${param}`);
  const isOpen = popover.classList.contains('open');
  document.querySelectorAll('.modulus-popover').forEach(p => p.classList.remove('open'));
  if (!isOpen) popover.classList.add('open');
}

function updateModulus(param, value) {
  const v = parseFloat(value);
  complexParams['r' + param] = v;
  const btn = document.getElementById(`mod-btn-${param}`);
  if (btn) btn.innerHTML = 'r<sub>' + param + '</sub>=' + v.toFixed(1);
  const popoverVal = document.getElementById(`popover-val-${param}`);
  if (popoverVal) popoverVal.textContent = v.toFixed(1);
  draw();
}

function stopAllAuto() {
  if (mode === 'RP1') {
    paramNames.forEach(name => {
      if (playTimers[name]) {
        clearInterval(playTimers[name]);
        delete playTimers[name];
        delete playDirections[name];
        const btn = document.getElementById(`play-${name}`);
        if (btn) { btn.textContent = '▶'; btn.classList.remove('active'); }
      }
      if (rewindTimers[name]) {
        clearInterval(rewindTimers[name]);
        delete rewindTimers[name];
        delete rewindDirections[name];
        const btn = document.getElementById(`rewind-${name}`);
        if (btn) { btn.textContent = '◀'; btn.classList.remove('active'); }
      }
    });
  } else {
    ['thetaA', 'thetaB', 'thetaC', 'thetaD'].forEach(name => {
      if (playTimers[name]) {
        clearInterval(playTimers[name]);
        delete playTimers[name];
        delete playDirections[name];
        const btn = document.getElementById(`play-${name}`);
        if (btn) { btn.textContent = '▶'; btn.classList.remove('active'); }
      }
      if (rewindTimers[name]) {
        clearInterval(rewindTimers[name]);
        delete rewindTimers[name];
        delete rewindDirections[name];
        const btn = document.getElementById(`rewind-${name}`);
        if (btn) { btn.textContent = '◀'; btn.classList.remove('active'); }
      }
    });
  }
}

function zoomAdjust(delta) {
  const slider = document.getElementById('zoom-slider');
  let newVal = parseFloat(slider.value) + delta;
  newVal = Math.max(-4, Math.min(3, newVal));
  slider.value = newVal;
  updateZoom();
}

function updateZoom() {
  const logZoom = parseFloat(document.getElementById('zoom-slider').value);
  zoomFactor = Math.pow(2, logZoom);
  updateView();
  document.getElementById('zoom-label').textContent = zoomFactor.toFixed(2) + '×';
  draw();
}

function resetView() {
  panX = 0;
  panY = 0;
  zoomFactor = 1;
  document.getElementById('zoom-slider').value = 0;
  updateView();
  document.getElementById('zoom-label').textContent = '1.00×';
  draw();
}

// ==================== 拖拽平移（修正旋转90度） ====================
let isDragging = false;
let lastMouseX = 0, lastMouseY = 0;
let dragPausedPlay = [];
let dragPausedRewind = [];
let isPinching = false;
let pinchStartDist = 0;
let pinchDrawPending = false;
let pinchPausedPlay = [];
let pinchPausedRewind = [];
let pinchStartZoom = 1;
let pinchCenterX = 0, pinchCenterY = 0;
let pinchStartPanX = 0, pinchStartPanY = 0;

canvas.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
  isDragging = true;
  dragPausedPlay = Object.keys(playTimers);
  dragPausedRewind = Object.keys(rewindTimers);
  stopAllAuto();
  lastMouseX = e.clientX;
  lastMouseY = e.clientY;
  canvas.style.cursor = 'grabbing';
});

canvas.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
  if (!isDragging) return;
  const dx = e.clientX - lastMouseX;
  const dy = e.clientY - lastMouseY;
  lastMouseX = e.clientX;
  lastMouseY = e.clientY;
  const range = xMax - xMin;
  const worldDx = -dy * range / (H - 2 * margin);
  const worldDy = -dx * range / (W - 2 * margin);
  panX += worldDx;
  panY += worldDy;
  updateView();
  draw();
});

canvas.addEventListener('mouseup', () => {
  isDragging = false;
  canvas.style.cursor = 'grab';
  for (const name of dragPausedPlay) {
    if (!playTimers[name]) {
      if (mode === 'RP1') togglePlay(name);
      else toggleCP1Play(name);
    }
  }
  for (const name of dragPausedRewind) {
    if (!rewindTimers[name]) {
      if (mode === 'RP1') toggleRewind(name);
      else toggleCP1Rewind(name);
    }
  }
  dragPausedPlay = [];
  dragPausedRewind = [];
});

canvas.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => {
  isDragging = false;
  canvas.style.cursor = 'grab';
  for (const name of dragPausedPlay) {
    if (!playTimers[name]) {
      if (mode === 'RP1') togglePlay(name);
      else toggleCP1Play(name);
    }
  }
  for (const name of dragPausedRewind) {
    if (!rewindTimers[name]) {
      if (mode === 'RP1') toggleRewind(name);
      else toggleCP1Rewind(name);
    }
  }
  dragPausedPlay = [];
  dragPausedRewind = [];
});

canvas.addEventListener('touchstart', (e) => {
  if (e.touches.length === 2) {
    isPinching = true;
    isDragging = false;
    pinchPausedPlay = Object.keys(playTimers);
    pinchPausedRewind = Object.keys(rewindTimers);
    stopAllAuto();
    const dx = e.touches[0].clientX - e.touches[1].clientX;
    const dy = e.touches[0].clientY - e.touches[1].clientY;
    pinchStartDist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
    pinchStartZoom = zoomFactor;
    pinchCenterX = (e.touches[0].clientX + e.touches[1].clientX) / 2;
    pinchCenterY = (e.touches[0].clientY + e.touches[1].clientY) / 2;
    pinchStartPanX = panX;
    pinchStartPanY = panY;
    e.preventDefault();
  } else if (e.touches.length === 1) {
    isDragging = true;
    dragPausedPlay = Object.keys(playTimers);
    dragPausedRewind = Object.keys(rewindTimers);
    stopAllAuto();
    lastMouseX = e.touches[0].clientX;
    lastMouseY = e.touches[0].clientY;
    e.preventDefault();
  }
}, { passive: false });

canvas.addEventListener('touchmove', (e) => {
  if (e.touches.length === 2 && isPinching) {
    const dx = e.touches[0].clientX - e.touches[1].clientX;
    const dy = e.touches[0].clientY - e.touches[1].clientY;
    const dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
    if (pinchStartDist > 0) {
      const scale = dist / pinchStartDist;
      const newZoom = pinchStartZoom * scale;
      zoomFactor = Math.max(0.0625, Math.min(8, newZoom));
      const logZoom = Math.log2(zoomFactor);
      document.getElementById('zoom-slider').value = logZoom;
      document.getElementById('zoom-label').textContent = zoomFactor.toFixed(2) + '×';
      updateView();
      draw();
    }
    e.preventDefault();
  } else if (isDragging && e.touches.length === 1) {
    const dx = e.touches[0].clientX - lastMouseX;
    const dy = e.touches[0].clientY - lastMouseY;
    lastMouseX = e.touches[0].clientX;
    lastMouseY = e.touches[0].clientY;
    const range = xMax - xMin;
    const worldDx = -dy * range / (H - 2 * margin);
    const worldDy = -dx * range / (W - 2 * margin);
    panX += worldDx;
    panY += worldDy;
    updateView();
    draw();
    e.preventDefault();
  }
}, { passive: false });

canvas.addEventListener('touchend', (e) => {
  if (e.touches.length < 2) {
    if (isPinching) {
      isPinching = false;
      for (const name of pinchPausedPlay) {
        if (!playTimers[name]) {
          if (mode === 'RP1') togglePlay(name);
          else toggleCP1Play(name);
        }
      }
      for (const name of pinchPausedRewind) {
        if (!rewindTimers[name]) {
          if (mode === 'RP1') toggleRewind(name);
          else toggleCP1Rewind(name);
        }
      }
      pinchPausedPlay = [];
      pinchPausedRewind = [];
    }
  }
  if (e.touches.length === 0) {
    isDragging = false;
    for (const name of dragPausedPlay) {
      if (!playTimers[name]) {
        if (mode === 'RP1') togglePlay(name);
        else toggleCP1Play(name);
      }
    }
    for (const name of dragPausedRewind) {
      if (!rewindTimers[name]) {
        if (mode === 'RP1') toggleRewind(name);
        else toggleCP1Rewind(name);
      }
    }
    dragPausedPlay = [];
    dragPausedRewind = [];
  }
});

function buildCurveParams() {
  const box = document.getElementById('curve-params-box');
  box.innerHTML = '';
  const defs = curveDefaults[currentCurve];
  if (!defs || Object.keys(defs).length === 0) {
    box.style.display = 'none';
    return;
  }
  box.style.display = 'flex';
  for (const key of Object.keys(defs)) {
    const row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'curve-param-row';
    const label = document.createElement('label');
    label.textContent = key + '=';
    const input = document.createElement('input');
    input.type = 'number';
    input.step = '0.1';
    input.value = curveParams[currentCurve][key];
    input.onchange = () => {
      curveParams[currentCurve][key] = parseFloat(input.value) || 0;
      draw();
    };
    row.appendChild(label);
    row.appendChild(input);
    box.appendChild(row);
  }
}

function changeCurve() {
  currentCurve = document.getElementById('curve-select').value;
  buildCurveParams();
  updateLegendColor();
  draw();
}

let dropdownOpen = false;
function toggleDropdown() {
  dropdownOpen = !dropdownOpen;
  document.getElementById('custom-dropdown').classList.toggle('open', dropdownOpen);
}

function selectCurve(value) {
  document.getElementById('curve-select').value = value;
  const trigger = document.getElementById('custom-select-trigger');
  const options = document.querySelectorAll('.custom-option');
  options.forEach(opt => {
    opt.classList.toggle('selected', opt.dataset.value === value);
    if (opt.dataset.value === value) {
      trigger.textContent = opt.textContent;
    }
  });
  dropdownOpen = false;
  document.getElementById('custom-dropdown').classList.remove('open');
  changeCurve();
}

// ==================== RP1 参数面板构建 ====================
function buildRP1Params() {
  const container = document.getElementById('param-container');
  container.innerHTML = '';
  paramNames.forEach(name => {
    const row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'param-row';
    row.style.gap = '4px';

    const label = document.createElement('span');
    label.className = 'param-label';
    label.textContent = name;
    label.style.width = '22px';

    const rewind = document.createElement('button');
    rewind.className = 'rewind-btn';
    rewind.id = `rewind-${name}`;
    rewind.textContent = '◀';
    rewind.onclick = () => toggleRewind(name);

    const minus = document.createElement('button');
    minus.className = 'btn btn-minus';
    minus.textContent = '−';
    minus.onmousedown = (e) => { e.preventDefault(); startAdjust(name, -1); };
    minus.onmouseup = (e) => { e.preventDefault(); stopAdjust(name, -1); };
    minus.onmouseleave = (e) => { stopAdjust(name, -1); };
    minus.ontouchstart = (e) => { e.preventDefault(); startAdjust(name, -1); };
    minus.ontouchend = (e) => { e.preventDefault(); stopAdjust(name, -1); };
    minus.ontouchcancel = (e) => { stopAdjust(name, -1); };

    const slider = document.createElement('input');
    slider.type = 'range';
    slider.min = VAL_MIN;
    slider.max = VAL_MAX;
    slider.step = stepSize;
    slider.value = params[name];
    slider.id = `slider-${name}`;
    slider.oninput = () => {
      params[name] = parseFloat(slider.value);
      const precision = stepSize < 0.05 ? 2 : (stepSize < 0.5 ? 1 : 0);
      document.getElementById(`val-${name}`).textContent = params[name].toFixed(precision);
      triggerSound(name, params[name]);
      draw();
    };

    const val = document.createElement('span');
    val.className = 'param-value';
    val.id = `val-${name}`;
    val.textContent = params[name].toFixed(0);
    val.style.width = '44px';

    const plus = document.createElement('button');
    plus.className = 'btn btn-plus';
    plus.textContent = '+';
    plus.onmousedown = (e) => { e.preventDefault(); startAdjust(name, 1); };
    plus.onmouseup = (e) => { e.preventDefault(); stopAdjust(name, 1); };
    plus.onmouseleave = (e) => { stopAdjust(name, 1); };
    plus.ontouchstart = (e) => { e.preventDefault(); startAdjust(name, 1); };
    plus.ontouchend = (e) => { e.preventDefault(); stopAdjust(name, 1); };
    plus.ontouchcancel = (e) => { stopAdjust(name, 1); };

    const play = document.createElement('button');
    play.className = 'play-btn';
    play.id = `play-${name}`;
    play.textContent = '▶';
    play.onclick = () => togglePlay(name);

    row.appendChild(label);
    row.appendChild(rewind);
    row.appendChild(minus);
    row.appendChild(slider);
    row.appendChild(val);
    row.appendChild(plus);
    row.appendChild(play);
    container.appendChild(row);
  });
}

// ==================== CP1 复数参数面板构建 ====================
function buildCP1Params() {
  const container = document.getElementById('param-container');
  container.innerHTML = '';
  const angleNames = ['thetaA', 'thetaB', 'thetaC', 'thetaD'];
  const labels = ['a', 'b', 'c', 'd'];

  angleNames.forEach((name, idx) => {
    const labelChar = labels[idx];
    const row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'param-row';
    row.style.gap = '2px';
    row.style.position = 'relative';

    const label = document.createElement('span');
    label.className = 'param-label';
    label.innerHTML = 'θ' + labelChar;
    label.style.width = '22px';
    label.style.fontSize = '12px';

    const rewind = document.createElement('button');
    rewind.className = 'rewind-btn';
    rewind.id = `rewind-${name}`;
    rewind.textContent = '◀';
    rewind.style.width = '24px';
    rewind.style.height = '24px';
    rewind.style.padding = '0';
    rewind.onclick = () => toggleCP1Rewind(name);

    const minus = document.createElement('button');
    minus.className = 'btn btn-minus';
    minus.textContent = '−';
    minus.style.width = '24px';
    minus.style.height = '24px';
    minus.style.padding = '0';
    minus.onmousedown = (e) => { e.preventDefault(); startCP1Adjust(name, -1); };
    minus.onmouseup = (e) => { e.preventDefault(); stopCP1Adjust(name, -1); };
    minus.onmouseleave = (e) => { stopCP1Adjust(name, -1); };
    minus.ontouchstart = (e) => { e.preventDefault(); startCP1Adjust(name, -1); };
    minus.ontouchend = (e) => { e.preventDefault(); stopCP1Adjust(name, -1); };
    minus.ontouchcancel = (e) => { stopCP1Adjust(name, -1); };

    const slider = document.createElement('input');
    slider.type = 'range';
    slider.min = 0;
    slider.max = 360;
    slider.step = stepSize;
    slider.value = complexParams[name];
    slider.id = `slider-${name}`;
    slider.oninput = () => {
      complexParams[name] = parseFloat(slider.value);
      const precision = stepSize < 0.05 ? 2 : (stepSize < 0.5 ? 1 : 0);
      document.getElementById(`val-${name}`).textContent = complexParams[name].toFixed(precision) + '°';
      playAngleSound(complexParams[name]);
      draw();
    };

    const val = document.createElement('span');
    val.className = 'param-value';
    val.id = `val-${name}`;
    val.textContent = complexParams[name].toFixed(0) + '°';
    val.style.width = '36px';
    val.style.fontSize = '11px';

    const plus = document.createElement('button');
    plus.className = 'btn btn-plus';
    plus.textContent = '+';
    plus.style.width = '24px';
    plus.style.height = '24px';
    plus.style.padding = '0';
    plus.onmousedown = (e) => { e.preventDefault(); startCP1Adjust(name, 1); };
    plus.onmouseup = (e) => { e.preventDefault(); stopCP1Adjust(name, 1); };
    plus.onmouseleave = (e) => { stopCP1Adjust(name, 1); };
    plus.ontouchstart = (e) => { e.preventDefault(); startCP1Adjust(name, 1); };
    plus.ontouchend = (e) => { e.preventDefault(); stopCP1Adjust(name, 1); };
    plus.ontouchcancel = (e) => { stopCP1Adjust(name, 1); };

    const play = document.createElement('button');
    play.className = 'play-btn';
    play.id = `play-${name}`;
    play.textContent = '▶';
    play.style.width = '24px';
    play.style.height = '24px';
    play.style.padding = '0';
    play.onclick = () => toggleCP1Play(name);

    const modWrap = document.createElement('div');
    modWrap.className = 'modulus-wrap';

    const modBtn = document.createElement('button');
    modBtn.className = 'modulus-btn';
    modBtn.id = `mod-btn-${labelChar}`;
    modBtn.innerHTML = 'r<sub>' + labelChar + '</sub>=' + complexParams['r' + labelChar].toFixed(1);
    modBtn.onclick = (e) => { e.stopPropagation(); toggleModulusPopover(labelChar); };

    const popover = document.createElement('div');
    popover.className = 'modulus-popover';
    popover.id = `popover-${labelChar}`;
    popover.innerHTML = `
      <div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:bold;color:#495057;">r<sub>${labelChar}</sub></div>
      <input type="range" orient="vertical" min="0" max="50" step="0.1"
             value="${complexParams['r' + labelChar]}"
             oninput="updateModulus('${labelChar}', this.value)">
      <div id="popover-val-${labelChar}" style="font-size:11px;color:#2e86de;font-weight:bold;">
        ${complexParams['r' + labelChar].toFixed(1)}
      </div>
    `;

    modWrap.appendChild(modBtn);
    modWrap.appendChild(popover);

    row.appendChild(label);
    row.appendChild(rewind);
    row.appendChild(minus);
    row.appendChild(slider);
    row.appendChild(val);
    row.appendChild(plus);
    row.appendChild(play);
    row.appendChild(modWrap);
    container.appendChild(row);
  });
}

function resetMobiusParams() {
  stopAllAuto();
  if (mode === 'RP1') {
    params = { a: 1, b: 0, c: 0, d: 1 };
    const precision = stepSize < 0.05 ? 2 : (stepSize < 0.5 ? 1 : 0);
    paramNames.forEach(name => {
      const slider = document.getElementById(`slider-${name}`);
      const val = document.getElementById(`val-${name}`);
      if (slider) slider.value = params[name];
      if (val) val.textContent = params[name].toFixed(precision);
    });
  } else {
    complexParams = {
      ra: 1, thetaA: 0,
      rb: 0, thetaB: 0,
      rc: 0, thetaC: 0,
      rd: 1, thetaD: 0
    };
    const precision = stepSize < 0.05 ? 2 : (stepSize < 0.5 ? 1 : 0);
    ['thetaA', 'thetaB', 'thetaC', 'thetaD'].forEach(name => {
      const slider = document.getElementById(`slider-${name}`);
      const val = document.getElementById(`val-${name}`);
      if (slider) slider.value = complexParams[name];
      if (val) val.textContent = complexParams[name].toFixed(precision) + '°';
    });
    ['a', 'b', 'c', 'd'].forEach(p => {
      const btn = document.getElementById(`mod-btn-${p}`);
      if (btn) btn.innerHTML = 'r<sub>' + p + '</sub>=' + complexParams['r' + p].toFixed(1);
      const popoverSlider = document.querySelector(`#popover-${p} input[type="range"]`);
      if (popoverSlider) popoverSlider.value = complexParams['r' + p];
      const popoverVal = document.getElementById(`popover-val-${p}`);
      if (popoverVal) popoverVal.textContent = complexParams['r' + p].toFixed(1);
    });
  }
  draw();
}

// 初始化
updateView();
buildCurveParams();
updateLegendColor();
setMode('RP1');

// 点击外部关闭下拉与模长 popover
document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
  if (!e.target.closest('.modulus-wrap')) {
    document.querySelectorAll('.modulus-popover').forEach(p => p.classList.remove('open'));
  }
  const wrap = document.getElementById('custom-select-wrap');
  if (wrap && !wrap.contains(e.target)) {
    dropdownOpen = false;
    document.getElementById('custom-dropdown').classList.remove('open');
  }
});
</script>
</body>
</html>
```

**主要改动说明：**

| 功能 | 实现 |
| --- | --- |

| **CP1 复数参数** | a=raeiθa ，角度 θ 滑条 0∼360∘ ，模长 r 按钮 0∼50 |
| --- | --- |
| **竖直模长滑块** | 点击 ra/rb/rc/rd 弹出竖直 `range`（`orient="vertical"`），点外部关闭 |
| **步进切换** | 自动带 `°` 符号， 0.01∘/0.1∘/1∘ |
| **循环播放** | 正放倒放均在 0∘↔360∘ 循环，不反弹 |
| **行列式检测** | CP1 下计算复数行列式模长 $ | ad-bc | $ |
| **奇点标记** | CP1 下计算复奇点 z=−d/c 的实部/虚部并标红 |
| **UI 分离** | RP1 与 CP1 切换时完全重建参数面板，状态互不干扰 |
| **拖拽恢复** | 拖拽画布后恢复自动播放时，自动判断当前模式调用对应播放函数 |

## 用户

把CP1下的角度步进改成0.1°，1°，10°。
以下是ds的修改建议，你判断一下是不是需要听从.
一、代码/架构层面的优化建议（针对现有 CSV）
1. 关于“模长 r 范围 0~50”的致命陷阱（必须修）
· 你允许 r 单独为 0（比如 r_c = 0 ），此时如果 r_a 和 r_d 不为 0，行列式 ad-bc 依然可能非零，这是合法的（退化为仿射变换）。
· 但是，当 r_c 无限接近 0 时，极点 z=-d/c 会直接飞向无穷远，导致绘图区域出现剧烈的数值抖动。
· 优化建议：在计算奇点 -d/c 和零点 -b/a 时，务必加上“防溢出截断”：如果分母的模长 < 10^{-12} ，直接将该点标记为“位于无穷远”（比如在画布边缘显示一个箭头），而不是算出一个巨大的垃圾数值导致画布崩溃。
2. 关于“椭圆曲线”的采样优化（图形保真）
· 椭圆曲线 y^2 = x^3 + px + q 在复平面上是环面，但在实投影下往往有两个分支。变换后，如果曲线经过极点 -d/c ，图像会“断裂”并延伸到无穷远。
· 优化建议：不要固定步长采样 t 。在计算 z'(t) 时，监测相邻两点的距离，如果突然超过阈值（比如画布宽度的 10 倍），立刻在断裂处停止连线，避免画出难看的“飞线”横跨整个屏幕。
3. 性能优化（播放时的卡顿）
· 你设置了 0.01^\circ 步进。如果每帧都重绘整条椭圆曲线（包含上千个点），浏览器会卡顿。
· 建议：采用 requestAnimationFrame 驱动播放，并设置一个“时间增量（deltaTime）”，确保每秒固定更新 30 帧即可，不必每度都刷新。

用户要求：

让我分析DS的三个建议：**建议1：模长r范围0~50的致命陷阱**

- 当r_c接近0时，极点z=-d/c会飞向无穷远，导致数值抖动
- 建议加防溢出截断：分母模长<1e-12时标记为无穷远
- **判断：需要听从。** 这是数值稳定性问题，确实会导致画布崩溃或画出巨大飞线。应该在transformPoint和drawSingularityMarkers中加入保护。

**建议2：椭圆曲线的采样优化**

- 建议监测相邻变换后点的距离，超过阈值时停止连线
- **判断：需要听从，但优先级中等。** 这确实是个问题，但不仅限于椭圆曲线，所有曲线在接近极点时都会有这个问题。更通用的方案是对所有曲线的transPts做距离检测。不过这是一个较大的改动，用户当前主要关注的是CP1复数参数改造，这个可以作为附加优化。

**建议3：性能优化**

- 建议用requestAnimationFrame，固定30fps
- **判断：部分听从。** 当前代码已经用setInterval(120ms)驱动播放，约8fps，其实并不算高。真正的问题是每次播放都调用draw()重绘所有4000个点。可以优化为：播放时只更新参数显示，用requestAnimationFrame做渲染循环。但这是一个架构改动，比较大。

综合来看：

- 建议1必须做，数值稳定性是基础
- 建议2可以做，对所有曲线transPts做距离截断
- 建议3可以简化做：不改架构，但播放间隔可以调整，或者保持现状（120ms已经够慢了）

等等，我需要仔细看看现有代码。drawCurvePath已经按idx分段了。问题是变换后，连续采样的点可能一个有限一个无穷大，或者两个都有限但中间跨过了极点。由于采样是参数t的连续变化，如果极点恰好在两个采样点之间，变换后的点会从一侧跳到另一侧，产生"飞线"。解决方案：在生成transPts时，检测相邻点的欧氏距离，如果超过阈值就断开。这个应该在transformPoint之后、drawCurvePath之前做。让我写代码：在draw()函数中：

JavaScript 复制

```javascript
const transPts = [];
for (let i = 0; i < origPts.length; i++) {
  const [x, y] = origPts[i];
  transPts.push(transformPoint(x, y));
}
// 新增：检测飞线并插入断点
const MAX_JUMP = 500; // 画布像素距离阈值
const processedPts = [];
for (let i = 0; i < transPts.length; i++) {
  const [x, y] = transPts[i];
  processedPts.push([x, y]);
  if (i < transPts.length - 1) {
    const [x2, y2] = transPts[i+1];
    if (isFinite(x) && isFinite(y) && isFinite(x2) && isFinite(y2)) {
      const dx = toCanvasX(x2) - toCanvasX(x);
      const dy = toCanvasY(y2) - toCanvasY(y);
      if (Math.sqrt(dx*dx + dy*dy) > MAX_JUMP) {
        processedPts.push([NaN, NaN]); // 插入断点
      }
    }
  }
}
```

如果我在transPts中插入NaN，那么origPts的索引i和transPts的索引就不再一一对应了。这会导致idx不连续。更好的做法：在drawCurvePath中增加距离检测，或者在生成transPts时做。实际上，最简单的方法是在drawCurvePath内部增加距离检测：

JavaScript 复制

```javascript
function drawCurvePath(pts, color, width, dash) {
  // ...
  let segments = [];
  let current = [valid[0]];
  for (let i = 1; i < valid.length; i++) {
    const prev = valid[i-1];
    const curr = valid[i];
    const isConnected = curr.idx === prev.idx + 1;
    let isClose = true;
    if (isConnected && isFinite(prev.x) && isFinite(prev.y) && isFinite(curr.x) && isFinite(curr.y)) {
      const dx = toCanvasX(curr.x) - toCanvasX(prev.x);
      const dy = toCanvasY(curr.y) - toCanvasY(prev.y);
      if (Math.sqrt(dx*dx + dy*dy) > 300) isClose = false; // 阈值
    }
    if (isConnected && isClose) {
      current.push(curr);
    } else {
      if (current.length > 1) segments.push(current);
      current = [curr];
    }
  }
  // ...
}
```

这样就不需要修改pts生成逻辑了。建议1的防溢出：
在mobiusComplex中：

JavaScript 复制

```javascript
function mobiusComplex(x, y, a_re, a_im, b_re, b_im, c_re, c_im, d_re, d_im) {
  const num_re = (a_re * x - a_im * y) + b_re;
  const num_im = (a_re * y + a_im * x) + b_im;
  const den_re = (c_re * x - c_im * y) + d_re;
  const den_im = (c_re * y + c_im * x) + d_im;
  const den_sq = den_re * den_re + den_im * den_im;
  if (den_sq < 1e-20) return [NaN, NaN];
  // 新增：防溢出
  const result_re = (num_re * den_re + num_im * den_im) / den_sq;
  const result_im = (num_im * den_re - num_re * den_im) / den_sq;
  if (!isFinite(result_re) || !isFinite(result_im) || Math.abs(result_re) > 1e6 || Math.abs(result_im) > 1e6) {
    return [NaN, NaN];
  }
  return [result_re, result_im];
}
```

在RP1的mobius中也可以加：

JavaScript 复制

```javascript
function mobius(z, a, b, c, d) {
  const denom = c * z + d;
  if (Math.abs(denom) < 1e-10) return NaN;
  const result = (a * z + b) / denom;
  if (!isFinite(result) || Math.abs(result) > 1e6) return NaN;
  return result;
}
```

JavaScript 复制

```javascript
function drawCurvePath(pts, color, width, dash) {
  ctx.strokeStyle = color;
  ctx.lineWidth = width;
  ctx.setLineDash(dash ? [6, 4] : []);

  let valid = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < pts.length; i++) {
    const [x, y] = pts[i];
    if (isFinite(x) && isFinite(y)) {
      valid.push({ x, y, idx: i });
    }
  }
  if (valid.length < 2) return;

  const MAX_JUMP = 250; // 像素距离阈值，超过则视为飞线断开

  let segments = [];
  let current = [valid[0]];
  for (let i = 1; i < valid.length; i++) {
    const prev = valid[i - 1];
    const curr = valid[i];
    let shouldBreak = false;
    if (curr.idx !== prev.idx + 1) {
      shouldBreak = true;
    } else {
      const dx = toCanvasX(curr.x) - toCanvasX(prev.x);
      const dy = toCanvasY(curr.y) - toCanvasY(prev.y);
      if (Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy) > MAX_JUMP) shouldBreak = true;
    }
    if (shouldBreak) {
      if (current.length > 1) segments.push(current);
      current = [curr];
    } else {
      current.push(curr);
    }
  }
  if (current.length > 1) segments.push(current);

  for (let seg of segments) {
    ctx.beginPath();
    for (let i = 0; i < seg.length; i++) {
      const cx = toCanvasX(seg[i].x);
      const cy = toCanvasY(seg[i].y);
      if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(cx, cy);
      else ctx.lineTo(cx, cy);
    }
    ctx.stroke();
  }
}
```

关于mobius的防溢出：

JavaScript 复制

```javascript
function mobius(z, a, b, c, d) {
  const denom = c * z + d;
  if (Math.abs(denom) < 1e-12) return NaN;
  const result = (a * z + b) / denom;
  if (!isFinite(result) || Math.abs(result) > 1e6) return NaN;
  return result;
}
```

关于mobiusComplex的防溢出：

JavaScript 复制

```javascript
function mobiusComplex(x, y, a_re, a_im, b_re, b_im, c_re, c_im, d_re, d_im) {
  const num_re = (a_re * x - a_im * y) + b_re;
  const num_im = (a_re * y + a_im * x) + b_im;
  const den_re = (c_re * x - c_im * y) + d_re;
  const den_im = (c_re * y + c_im * x) + d_im;
  const den_sq = den_re * den_re + den_im * den_im;
  if (den_sq < 1e-20) return [NaN, NaN];
  const X = (num_re * den_re + num_im * den_im) / den_sq;
  const Y = (num_im * den_re - num_re * den_im) / den_sq;
  if (!isFinite(X) || !isFinite(Y) || Math.abs(X) > 1e6 || Math.abs(Y) > 1e6) return [NaN, NaN];
  return [X, Y];
}
```


data-step是0.1, 1, 10。updateStepButtons会去掉°再加°，这没问题。setStep函数中：

JavaScript 复制

```javascript
document.querySelectorAll('.step-btn').forEach(btn => {
  const clean = btn.dataset.step.replace(/°/g, '');
  btn.classList.toggle('active', parseFloat(clean) === s);
});
```

CP1下的precision：

JavaScript 复制

```javascript
const precision = stepSize < 1 ? 1 : 0;
```

这样0.1显示1位小数，1和10显示0位小数。在buildCP1Params中创建slider时：

JavaScript 复制

```javascript
slider.step = stepSize;
```

在adjustCP1Param中：

JavaScript 复制

```javascript
const precision = stepSize < 1 ? 1 : 0;
```

在toggleCP1Play/toggleCP1Rewind中：

JavaScript 复制

```javascript
const precision = stepSize < 1 ? 1 : 0;
```

所以`input[type="range"]`应该能选到。还有一个问题：在buildCP1Params中，popover里的input oninput直接调updateModulus，但updateModulus函数中：

JavaScript 复制

```javascript
function updateModulus(param, value) {
  const v = parseFloat(value);
  complexParams['r' + param] = v;
  const btn = document.getElementById(`mod-btn-${param}`);
  if (btn) btn.innerHTML = 'r<sub>' + param + '</sub>=' + v.toFixed(1);
  const popoverVal = document.getElementById(`popover-val-${param}`);
  if (popoverVal) popoverVal.textContent = v.toFixed(1);
  draw();
}
```

这没问题。现在让我输出。

三个建议的判断如下：

| 建议 | 是否听从 | 理由 |
| --- | --- | --- |

| **1. 防溢出截断** | ✅ **必须听从** | 当 rc→0 时极点飞向无穷远，不截断会产生 `1e308` 级别的垃圾坐标，直接导致 Canvas 崩溃或画出贯穿全屏的飞线。这是数值稳定性底线。 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **2. 飞线检测（距离阈值）** | ✅ **听从** | 不仅椭圆曲线，所有曲线在掠过极点时都会出现相邻采样点从 −∞ 跳到 +∞ 的飞线。在 `drawCurvePath` 中加入画布像素距离检测即可通用解决。 |
| **3. requestAnimationFrame** | ⚠️ **暂不实施** | 当前播放已用 `120ms` 间隔（约 8fps），对浏览器负担不大。引入 `rAF` 需要重构整个动画架构，收益与复杂度不成正比，后续如需 60fps 流畅播放再改不迟。 |

下面是整合后的完整文件，包含：

1. 步进改为 **0.1° / 1° / 10°**
2. `mobius` / `mobiusComplex` 增加溢出截断（`|result| > 10^6` 视为无穷远）
3. `drawCurvePath` 增加飞线检测（相邻点画布距离超过 250px 自动断开）
4. 奇点标记增加更严格的模长阈值保护
